By mimicking the shape of the fingertip and fingernail found on a human finger, we develop a biomimetic multimodal haptic fingertip and validate it on selected guqin string-contact tasks, including open-string and stopped-note comparisons, harmonic-tuning, and tactile-triggered bimanual coordination, using the guqin, a traditional Chinese musical instrument, as a challenging validation scenario rather than as a fully demonstrated robotic performance system. This research integrates tactile sensing with robotics technology, thereby contributing to applications in world heritage conservation and cultural dissemination.

</details>


### [99] [Benchmarking and Reasoning Distillation of Large Language Models for Feedback Controller Design in Complex Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07004)
*Zhongchao Zhou,Yixuan Xie,Wenwei Yu,Yuxi Lu,Yaonan Zhu,Qian Niu,Yutaka Matsuo,Yusuke Iwasawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CoDyControlBench基准，包含132个系统配置、五个评估维度，用于评估LLM在复杂反馈控制器设计任务中的能力。六种主流LLM被测试，GPT成功率最高（94.8%），Qwen最低（50.0%）。此外，通过推理蒸馏开发了1.5B参数的轻量级边缘部署模型，在气动人工肌肉驱动的机械臂物理试验中成功实现目标跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在科学领域的控制器设计评估存在两个主要局限：基准过于集中于线性单自由度系统和大型API托管模型，缺乏对复杂控制器设计任务性能及边缘部署可行性的评估。为解决这些问题，作者提出了涵盖多自由度、多种系统类型等的全面基准。

Method: 构建CoDyControlBench基准，包含132个系统配置，沿五个维度展开：自由度数、系统类型、耦合级别、阻尼领域和控制器类型。评估了六个SOTA LLM（三个商业模型：GPT、Gemini、Claude；三个开源模型：GLM、DeepSeek、Qwen），进行三次独立运行。同时通过推理蒸馏方法开发了1.5B参数的轻量级边缘部署模型。

Result: GPT以94.8%的设计成功率居首，Qwen为50.0%。自由度数（变化范围36.3%）和控制器类型（17.6%）是对设计成功率影响最大的维度。GPT与Qwen的性能差异主要源于控制设计知识，特别是增益选择和瞬态限制机制的使用。推理蒸馏的1.5B模型在基准上优于答案蒸馏和基础模型，在1-6自由度上保持稳定性能，并在气动人工肌肉机械臂的三次物理试验中均实现成功目标跟踪。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了LLM控制器设计任务的基准基线，并展示了轻量级、可边缘部署的控制器设计模型的潜力，为未来在该领域的研究提供了参考基准和可行方向。

Abstract: Although remarkable capabilities have been demonstrated by Large Language Models (LLMs) across scientific domains, feedback controller design remains underexplored. Existing benchmarks focus mainly on linear single-Degree-of-Freedom (DoF) systems and large API-hosted models, leaving performance on complex controller-design tasks and feasibility for edge deployment unclear. To address these limitations, we introduce the Complex Dynamics-to-Control Benchmark for Large Language Models (CoDyControlBench), comprising 132 system configurations across five evaluation dimensions: number of DoF, system type, coupling level, damping regime, and controller type. Six state-of-the-art LLMs were evaluated over three independent runs, including three commercial models (GPT, Gemini, and Claude) and three open-source models (GLM, DeepSeek, and Qwen). GPT achieved the highest design success rate at 94.8\%, whereas Qwen showed the lowest rate at 50.0\%. Across the benchmark dimensions, DoF and controller type exhibited the largest model-averaged variations in design success, with success-rate ranges of 36.3\% and 17.6\%, respectively, exceeding those associated with system type, coupling level, and damping regime. Comparison of GPT and Qwen showed that their performance gap arose mainly from the control-design knowledge, particularly gain selection and the use of transient-limiting mechanisms. For edge deployment, a specialized 1.5B-parameter model was developed through reasoning distillation. The reasoning-distilled model outperformed the answer-distilled and base model on CoDyControlBench, maintained stable performance across 1-6 DoFs, and achieved successful traget tracking in all three physical trials on a pneumatic-artificial-muscle-driven robotic arm. These results establish a benchmark baseline and highlight the potential of lightweight, edge-deployable controller-design models.

</details>


### [100] [Real-time Whole-Body Motion Planning for Mobile Manipulators Carrying Arbitrarily Shaped Payloads via Kinematically-Coupled SVSDF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07005)
*Yisheng Li,Longji Yin,Tingrui Zhang,Ruize Xue,Haoda Zhu,Nan Chen,Siqi Liang,Yuxi Liu,Fu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向任意形状大件负载的移动机械臂实时全身运动规划框架，结合链式分解核碰撞检测前端、连续轨迹预处理中端和基于运动学耦合SVSDF的轨迹优化后端，可高效通过杂乱环境中的狭窄通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规划器要么过度简化负载几何形状，要么未处理机械臂连杆间的运动学耦合，导致可行空间丢失或优化停滞，难以应对携带大型非凸负载的移动机械臂在杂乱环境中的运输任务。

Method: 前端采用链式分解的基于核的碰撞检测方法，保留机器人与负载的真实几何，通过紧凑存储和快速位级查询实现高效检测；中端预处理阶段将前端路径转换为连续轨迹以保障平滑性和可行性，若无碰撞则直接执行以绕过昂贵的后端优化；后端则基于运动学耦合的SVSDF（KC-SVSDF）进行轨迹优化，沿运动学链传播避障梯度，生成连贯的全身逃逸方向。

Result: 消融实验、与最先进基线的对比基准以及在差速驱动移动机械臂上的真实世界实验表明，该框架能够可靠地在狭窄通道和杂乱环境中运输大型非凸负载。

Conclusion: 该框架通过三级流水线设计（前端碰撞检测、中端预处理、后端优化），实现了真实几何保持、运动学耦合处理与实时性的统一，为移动机械臂携带任意形状大件负载的全身运动规划提供了有效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Mobile manipulators are increasingly tasked with transporting large, non-convex payloads through cluttered environments, yet existing planners either oversimplify the payload geometry or fail to handle the kinematic coupling between manipulator links, leading to lost feasible space or stalled optimization. This letter presents a real-time whole-body motion planning framework for mobile manipulators carrying arbitrarily shaped payloads. The front-end employs a chain-decomposed kernel-based collision check that preserves the true geometry of the robot and payload, with compact storage and fast bit-level queries. A mid-end preprocessing stage converts the front-end path into a continuous trajectory enforcing smoothness and feasibility, and executes it directly when collision-free to bypass the costly back-end. When refinement is required, the back-end performs trajectory optimization built on a Kinematically-Coupled SVSDF (KC-SVSDF), which propagates collision-avoidance gradients along the kinematic chain to produce coherent whole-body escape directions. Ablation studies, comparative benchmarks against state-of-the-art baselines, and real-world experiments on a differential-drive mobile manipulator demonstrate that the proposed framework reliably transports large, non-convex payloads through tight passages and cluttered environments.

</details>


### [101] [C2Dex: Contact-Consistent Reconstruction and Retargeting for Dexterous Manipulation from Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07045)
*Jie Ren,Zhehao Jiang,Yinhong Yang,Haorui Jia,Han Jiang,Ben Li,Yao Yao,Cheng Lin,Qiu Shen,Zhenshan Bing,Xiao-Xiao Long,Xun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: C2Dex提出了一种基于共享交互表征（稳定的物体侧接触点）的视频到灵巧操作框架，通过聚合单目视频中逐帧噪声观测来恢复稳定的物体接触，并将其同时用作轨迹级重建约束和跨实体迁移目标，在DexYCB和TACO数据集上显著超越基线，并通过真实机器人回放验证了物理可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧机器人操作的高质量演示数据昂贵且难以收集，而单目人类视频提供了可扩展的多样化操作行为来源。然而，将此类演示迁移到灵巧机器人面临两大挑战：单目手-物交互（HOI）重建常产生时间上不稳定的接触和物理上不合理的交互；传统重定向方法难以在不同手部实体间保持任务相关的接触和局部交互几何。

Method: C2Dex围绕一个共享交互表征构建，即在规范物体空间中聚合逐帧噪声观测以恢复稳定的物体侧接触。这些稳定接触具有双重作用：(1)作为轨迹级约束，引导人类HOI重建趋向时间连贯且物理合理；(2)作为灵巧手的显式迁移目标，通过拉普拉斯交互优化保持跨实体的局部手-物几何，并通过残差强化学习在仿真中细化轨迹。

Result: 在DexYCB和TACO数据集上，C2Dex的端到端轨迹成功率分别达到57.78%和26.67%，显著优于最强基线（17.78%和10.00%）。真实机器人回放实验进一步证明了其在多种接触密集型操作任务中的物理可行性。

Conclusion: C2Dex通过稳定的物体侧接触表征同时解决了重建不稳定性和跨实体迁移难题，验证了视频到灵巧操作学习的可行路径，并为从大规模单目人类视频中学习多样化灵巧操作行为提供了有效框架。

Abstract: High-quality demonstrations for dexterous robot manipulation are costly and difficult to collect, whereas monocular human videos provide a scalable source of diverse manipulation behaviors. However, transferring such demonstrations to dexterous robots remains challenging: monocular hand-object interaction (HOI) reconstruction often produces temporally unstable contacts and physically implausible interactions, while conventional retargeting methods struggle to preserve task-relevant contacts and local interaction geometry across different hand embodiments. We present C2Dex, a video-to-dexterous-manipulation framework built around a shared interaction representation: stable object-side contacts recovered by aggregating noisy frame-wise observations in the canonical object space. These stable contacts serve a dual role: as trajectory-level constraints that guide reconstruction toward temporally coherent and physically plausible human HOI trajectories, and as explicit transfer targets for the dexterous hand, where Laplacian interaction optimization preserves the local hand-object geometry across embodiments and residual reinforcement learning refines the trajectory in simulation. Experiments on DexYCB and TACO show that C2Dex achieves end-to-end trajectory success rates of 57.78% and 26.67%, respectively, substantially outperforming the strongest baselines (17.78% and 10.00%) under identical evaluation criteria. Real-robot replay experiments further demonstrate physical feasibility across diverse contact-rich manipulation tasks. Project page: https://k-jie.github.io/C2Dex/

</details>


### [102] [AutoIntervene: Calibrated Intervention for Action-Chunking Imitation Learning Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07065)
*Jinhe Tang,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AutoIntervene 是一个在线框架，通过在动作分块策略与操作员之间选择性切换控制权来应对部署中的感知错误和执行漂移。它利用视觉-动作支持记忆评估提议的动作块，并采用了阶段局部和全局两种支持机制来分别控制策略到操作员和操作员到策略的切换，并通过经验分位数自动校准阈值。实验表明该方法在真实双手操作任务中相比手动干预获得了更高的任务成功率和更低的操作员控制时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作分块视运动策略虽然通过预测短动作序列改善了时间一致性，但在部署中会因感知错误和执行漂移而偏离演示分布，此时策略仍会继续生成平滑但与当前状态不一致的动作块，缺乏有效的在线纠错机制。

Method: 构建视觉-动作支持记忆（来自成功任务执行的累积数据），将视觉相似性与提议动作-参考动作一致性相结合来评估候选动作块。采用阶段局部支持来决定策略向操作员转移（当前任务阶段内），全局支持决定操作员恢复后策略重新接管。从留出专家演示的评分经验分位数中分别校准两个方向的切换阈值，避免手动调参。保留成功滚动中的干预段作为校正监督用于后续策略更新。

Result: 在真实世界的双手操作任务上，相比手动干预，该方法取得了更高的任务成功率和更低的操作员控制时间。

Conclusion: AutoIntervene 通过基于支持记忆的自动干预机制，在策略与操作员之间实现自适应控制切换，既保证了任务成功率，又减少了对人工的依赖，并能利用干预数据进一步改进策略。

Abstract: Action-chunking visuomotor policies learn from demonstrations and improve temporal consistency by predicting short action sequences rather than single-step commands. Yet perception errors and execution drift can move the robot outside the demonstration distribution, while the policy continues to produce smooth action chunks that are inconsistent with the observed state. We present AutoIntervene, an online framework that selectively transfers control between an action-chunking policy and an operator during deployment. AutoIntervene evaluates proposed chunks against a visual-action support memory built from successful task executions, combining visual similarity with consistency between proposed and reference actions. Phase-local support governs policy-to-operator transfer within the current task phase, whereas global support governs the return to policy control after operator recovery. We calibrate separate switching thresholds for the two directions from empirical quantiles of evaluation-level scores on held-out expert demonstrations, avoiding direct manual tuning of score cutoffs. Intervention segments retained from successful rollouts target learner-induced states and provide corrective supervision for subsequent policy updates. Experiments on real-world bimanual manipulation tasks show higher post-adaptation task success and lower operator-control time than manual intervention. Videos and additional results are available at https://aus.bot/research/autointervene/.

</details>


### [103] [M2-SMap: Memory-Efficient Semantic Mapping with Hierarchical Multi-Model Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07074)
*QiYing Deng,ZhongLai Wang,Yuan Gao,Wei Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: M2-SMap是一个基于分层多模型表示的内存高效语义建图框架，通过将RGB-D点云分解为高斯组件、语义标注和多尺度特征提取，分别用有界平面、物体级超二次曲面和GMM基元表示大平面区域、语义物体和复杂残留结构，在保持实时性能的同时显著降低了内存占用并消除了物体间粘连。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集点云地图在资源受限机器人上内存消耗随场景规模快速增长；紧凑单模型表示几何表达能力固定，难以适应结构多样的环境；现有多模型方法在特征提取和模型选择上过度依赖局部几何，导致过拟合和物体间粘连问题。

Method: 提出M2-SMap框架，包括四个核心组件：(1) 分层几何分解将RGB-D点云划分为紧凑高斯组件；(2) 投影引导的语义标注机制为每个组件分配实例身份；(3) 将语义标注融入物体感知的高斯融合策略；(4) 多尺度特征提取策略将大平面区域、语义物体和复杂残留结构分别表示为有界平面、物体级超二次曲面和GMM基元。

Result: 在三个RGB-D序列上的实验表明，M2-SMap以不低于29.37 Hz的实时速度运行，实现了最低的基元数量，相比最佳基线平均减少18.7%；同时将每帧平均物体间粘连案例从2.808降至0，展示了高效且语义一致的场景表示能力。

Conclusion: M2-SMap通过层次化多模型表示和语义感知的建模策略，在保证实时性的同时有效解决了内存效率与几何表达能力之间的平衡问题，并完全消除了物体间粘连，为资源受限机器人提供了一种高效且语义一致的场景建图方案。

Abstract: Dense point cloud maps, as a typically used mapping representation, are difficult to deploy on resource-constrained robots because their memory consumption grows rapidly with scene scale. Although compact single-model representations reduce memory cost, their fixed geometric expressiveness is insufficient for structurally diverse environments. Existing multi-model methods improve representational flexibility, yet their feature extraction and model selection are often dominated by local geometry, which can cause overfitting and adhesion between objects. To address these issues, this paper presents M2-SMap, a memory-efficient semantic mapping framework based on hierarchical multi-model representation. First, a hierarchical geometric decomposition partitions RGB-D point clouds into compact Gaussian components. Then, a projection-guided semantic annotation mechanism assigns instance identities to each component. Subsequently, these annotations are incorporated into an object-aware Gaussian fusion strategy. Furthermore, a multi-scale feature extraction strategy separates large planar regions, semantic objects, and complex residual structures, which are respectively represented by bounded planes, object-level superquadrics, and GMM primitives. Experiments on three RGB-D sequences show that M2-SMap runs in real time at no less than 29.37 Hz while achieving the lowest primitive count, with an average reduction of 18.7% over the best baseline. It also reduces the mean per-frame number of measured inter-object adhesion cases from 2.808 to 0, demonstrating efficient and semantically consistent scene representation.

</details>


### [104] [Detection and Ranging of Transient Extrinsic Contacts Based on 6D Dynamic Tactile Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07075)
*Haowen Zheng,Yinghao Wu,Fuyuan Liu,Yichen Li,Yitian Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TECDAR，一种利用6D IMU动态触觉传感在7kHz采样率下检测和定位被抓取物体与外部环境瞬态接触的方法，通过扩展卡尔曼滤波融合触觉与机器人位姿，可在180ms内实现毫米级（约7mm）的接触定位精度，使机器人能在毫秒尺度内修正轨迹，实现精确工具操作和环境感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精细操作经常涉及被抓物体与环境之间短暂而微妙的碰撞，人手凭借优越的触觉灵敏度可轻松定位这些接触，但机器人系统常缺乏所需的分辨率来获取运动规划必要的信息，导致操作笨拙甚至任务失败。

Method: 提出瞬态外部接触检测与测距（TECDAR）方法。夹爪指尖采用单个2.5×3 mm 6D惯性测量单元进行动态触觉传感，以7kHz采样率捕获亚毫秒级指尖变形，数据流仅84 KB/s。通过扩展卡尔曼滤波融合触觉数据与机器人位姿，实现对被抓物体与周围环境之间外部接触的快速精确定位。

Result: 系统在180ms内实现毫米级定位精度，在线接触和点接触定位任务中平均定位精度约为7mm。近瞬时定位使机器人能在毫秒尺度上修正轨迹，实现精确工具操作与复杂环境感知。

Conclusion: TECDAR有望推动需要精细操作的机器人领域发展，包括精密装配、手术辅助以及以触觉为主导环境中的自主探索。

Abstract: Delicate manipulation often involves transient and subtle collisions between a grasped object and the environment. While the human hand localizes these contacts effortlessly thanks to superior tactile sensitivity, robotic systems often lack the requisite resolution to acquire the information necessary for motion planning, resulting in clumsy manipulation or even task failure. Here, we propose transient extrinsic contact detection and ranging (TECDAR), a simple yet fast and efficient method for detecting and ranging extrinsic contact of grasped objects. Our design of gripper tips employs dynamic tactile sensing leveraging a single 2.5$\times$3 mm 6D inertial measurement unit. The sensor captures sub-millisecond tip deformations at a 7 kHz sampling rate, but operating on a data stream of only 84 KB/s. High bandwidth and compact data size enable the system to rapidly detect and localize contact between grasped objects and their surroundings. Specifically, fusing tactile data with robot pose via an extended Kalman filter enables fast and precise localization of extrinsic contact, reaching millimeter-level accuracy within 180 ms. Experimental results demonstrate that the system achieves an average localization accuracy of approximately 7\,mm in both line-contact and point-contact localization tasks. Furthermore, this near-instantaneous localization enables the robot to rectify its trajectory on a millisecond scale, facilitating precise tool manipulation and enhanced perception of complex environments purely through tactile exploration and mapping. We envision such techniques advancing the future of robotics across domains requiring delicate manipulation, including precision assembly, surgical assistance, and autonomous exploration in touch-dominant environments. Project page: humitlab.github.io/TECDAR/

</details>


### [105] [LifelongCrossNav: Persistent 3D Semantic Memory for Cross-Floor Multi-Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07079)
*Zehui Li,Zihao Sun,Jiawei Xu,Zheqi He,Xiaoqiang Zhang,Jing-Shu Zheng,Lu Liu,Dahui Gao,Xiuwan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 LifelongCrossNav 框架，用于未知多楼层室内环境中的顺序多目标导航任务。该框架通过共享稀疏 3D 语义体素记忆持续累积几何结构、可通行状态和视觉语言特征，结合支持感知的 3D 可通行性建图、楼梯感知与方向感知的跨楼层导航，并引入 HM3D-MFMON 基准数据集。实验表明其在多楼层多目标导航任务中优于平面持续性语义地图基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标导航在语义感知和探索方面已取得显著进展，但多目标导航的持久记忆与跨楼层导航通常被分开研究。现有方法缺乏统一的框架来同时处理顺序多目标查询和跨楼层场景中的持续性环境记忆问题。

Method: LifelongCrossNav 框架包含：1) 共享稀疏 3D 语义体素记忆，增量累积几何结构、可通行状态和视觉语言特征；2) 支持感知的 3D 可通行性建图、楼梯特定感知和方向感知的楼梯穿越；3) 统一导航策略，协调同层前沿探索、实时与历史兴趣点检索、楼梯导航及目标搜索接近；4) 提出基于 HM3D 场景的 HM3D-MFMON 基准，含需要至少一次楼层切换的子集。

Result: 在 HM3D-MFMON 基准上，LifelongCrossNav 一致性地优于代表性平面持久语义地图基线，验证了持久 3D 语义记忆与跨楼层可通行性建模对顺序多目标导航的有效性。

Conclusion: 持久 3D 语义记忆和跨楼层可通行性建模能够有效地支持多楼层环境中的顺序多目标导航任务，同时新提出的 HM3D-MFMON 基准为该方向提供了评测基础。

Abstract: Object-goal navigation has made substantial progress in semantic perception and exploration, yet persistent memory for multi-object navigation and cross-floor navigation are still commonly addressed separately. We present LifelongCrossNav, a framework for sequential multi-object ObjectNav in unknown multi-floor indoor environments. Within each episode, the agent receives an ordered sequence of object-goal queries while continuously maintaining a shared sparse 3D semantic voxel memory. This memory incrementally accumulates geometric structure, traversability states, and vision-language features, allowing subsequent object-goal queries to retrieve previously acquired scene information without rebuilding the map. To support persistent search across floors, LifelongCrossNav combines support-aware 3D traversability mapping, stair-specific perception, and direction-aware stair traversal. A unified navigation policy coordinates same-floor frontier exploration, live and historical point-of-interest retrieval, stair navigation, and target-object search and approach. We further introduce HM3D-MFMON, a benchmark for sequential Multi-Floor Multi-Object Navigation built on HM3D scenes, including a dedicated subset in which completing the full sequence of object-goal subtasks requires at least one floor transition. Experimental results show that LifelongCrossNav consistently outperforms a representative planar persistent semantic-map baseline on HM3D-MFMON, demonstrating that persistent 3D semantic memory and cross-floor traversability modeling effectively support sequential multi-object navigation in multi-floor environments. Project page: https://flageval-baai.github.io/LifelongCrossNavPage.

</details>


### [106] [Identifying the Key Biomechanical Features of Movement Adaptation during Exoskeleton-Assisted Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07140)
*Peter Seungjune Lee,Katja Mombaur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究外骨骼辅助行走过程中人类自然适应的时间演化特征，发现适应过程是渐进的、高度个体化的，代谢反应异质且常不收敛，强调了个体层面时间演化分析的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前对外骨骼辅助运动过程中人类自然适应的理解有限，尤其是个体差异和时间进程方面的认识不足，需要更深入的研究来揭示适应机制。

Method: 9名健康参与者在三种条件下（无外骨骼、外骨骼主动踝关节助力、外骨骼零力矩）进行跑步机行走，分别从群体和个体层面分析下肢运动学、关节间协调性和运动代谢成本（MCoT）。

Result: 适应是渐进且高度个体化的，参与者在收敛时间和运动模式上存在显著差异；运动学适应在下肢不同关节间异步发生，摆动期波动更大；代谢响应异质且常不收敛，挑战了文献中常用的稳态假设。

Conclusion: 强调个体层面的时间演化分析对于理解外骨骼使用中的适应动态至关重要，传统群体水平的稳态分析方法不足以捕捉适应过程的复杂性。

Abstract: The understanding of natural human adaptation during exoskeleton-assisted locomotion - particularly individual differences in adaptation behaviors and temporal progression - remains limited. In this work, we investigate temporal evolution of biomechanical variables to uncover participant-specific adaptation strategies across different exoskeleton-assisted locomotion scenarios. Nine healthy participants performed treadmill walking under three conditions: without an exoskeleton, with exoskeleton active ankle assistance, and with exoskeleton zero-torque. Lower limb kinematics, inter-joint coordination, and metabolic cost of transport (MCoT) were analyzed at both the group and individual levels. Results indicate that adaptation is gradual and highly individualized, with substantial variability in convergence timing and movement patterns across participants. Kinematic adaptation occurred asynchronously across lower limb, with larger fluctuations during the swing phase. Metabolic responses were heterogeneous and often non-convergent, highlighting the limitations of steady-state assumptions commonly adopted in the literature. These findings emphasize the importance of individual-level, temporal evolution analyses for understanding adaptation dynamics in exoskeleton use.

</details>


### [107] [Representation Handoffs for OpenArm-Based Laboratory Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07154)
*Yang Shen,Chonghao Cheng,Ziyi Zhao,Jialuo Zhu,Zhenyi Yi,Qi Zhao,Jian Yang,Yuhui Shi,Chin-Teng Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了一个基于OpenArm的双臂移动机器人实验室自动化原型系统，通过表示交接（representation handoffs）将自然语言指令、传感器观测、对象先验和运行时绑定组织为可执行的运动目标，并利用干跑追踪和启动检查来识别部署瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源机器人学和基础模型降低了具身AI的准入门槛，但语言引导的实验室自动化仍需将指令和观测可靠地对齐到安全动作上。现有方案缺乏系统化的中间表示来调试语言、感知、规划与安全之间的集成问题。

Method: 构建了一个OpenArm双臂移动机器人原型，集成了移动基座、垂直滑轨、RGB-D传感、激光雷达建图、ROS2/MoveIt执行以及配置文件定义的技能接口。系统围绕四种表示交接组织：自然语言请求约束为注册技能调用、传感器观测接地为地图和对象位姿、对象先验提供角色和技能约束、运行时绑定将验证过的技能编译为可执行运动目标。评估采用干跑追踪和启动检查。

Result: 该集成路径揭示了系统暴露的明确部署阻塞项，包括缺失的校准、不完整的对象资产以及未完成真实场景视觉接地。这些中间表示被证明是实用调试接口。

Conclusion: 中间表示层可作为集成语言、感知、规划和机器人安全的实用调试接口，为实验室类任务的具身自动化系统提供可验证、可追踪的部署路径。

Abstract: Open-source robotics and foundation models have lowered the barrier to embodied AI, yet language-guided laboratory automation still requires reliable alignment from instructions and observations to safe actions. This field report presents an OpenArm-based mobile manipulation prototype for laboratory-style tasks, built by integrating dual OpenArm manipulators with a mobile base, vertical slide, RGB-D sensing, lidar-based mapping, ROS2/MoveIt execution, and profile-defined skill interfaces. The system is organized around representation handoffs: natural language requests are constrained into registered skill calls, sensor observations are grounded into maps and object poses, object priors provide role and skill constraints, and runtime bindings compile validated skills into executable motion goals. We use dry-run traces and startup checks to evaluate this integration path, showing how the prototype exposes missing calibration, incomplete object assets, and unfinished real-scene visual grounding as explicit deployment blockers. These intermediate representations serve as practical debugging interfaces for integrating language, perception, planning, and robot safety in embodied systems.

</details>


### [108] [TEMPO: Semantic-Action Decoupled RL Post-Training for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07314)
*Ziheng Liu,Quantao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TEMPO提出了一种语义-动作解耦的双时间尺度强化学习后训练框架，冻结VLA模型的视觉-语言骨干网络，分别以不同频率更新语义投影层和低层动作专家，在CALVIN基准和真实机器人操作任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型后训练方式存在明显不足：SFT容易导致分布不匹配问题；而现有RL后训练方法对所有模型组件采用统一的更新策略，忽略了不同组件（如语义表示层和动作执行层）在功能上的差异，无法在保持语义稳定性和快速学习动作控制之间取得平衡。

Method: TEMPO框架采用语义-动作解耦的双时间尺度RL后训练策略。具体地：1）冻结预训练的视觉-语言骨干网络以保留通用语义表示；2）将适应范围限制在两个组件上——语义投影层和低层动作专家，并为它们设置独立的RL优化循环；3）语义投影层低频更新以保持潜在动作特征稳定，动作专家高频更新以从在线交互中快速融入控制反馈。这种解耦策略防止快速策略更新破坏高层语义表示，同时保证动作专家高效学习。

Result: 在CALVIN基准和真实世界操作任务上的实验表明，TEMPO一致性地超越了预训练SOTA VLA模型以及RL后训练基线，在两个真实世界任务上能够达到并维持更高的评估奖励。

Conclusion: 语义-动作解耦的双时间尺度RL后训练策略是有效的：通过差异化更新VLA模型中不同功能组件，可以在保持语义表示稳定的同时高效学习动作控制，为VLA模型的RL后训练提供了一种更精细化的优化范式。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are commonly adapted to downstream manipulation tasks via supervised fine-tuning (SFT) or online reinforcement learning (RL) post-training. SFT is prone to distribution mismatch, and existing RL approaches typically apply a single, uniform update strategy to all model components, ignoring their distinct functional roles. We propose TEMPO, a semantic-action decoupled, two-timescale RL post-training framework for VLA models. TEMPO freezes the pretrained vision-language backbone to preserve general semantic representations, and restricts adaptation to two components with dedicated RL optimization loops: the semantic projection layer and the low-level action expert. We update them at different rates--the semantic projection layer infrequently, to keep the latent action stable, and the action expert frequently, to rapidly incorporate control feedback from online interaction. This decoupling RL fine-tuning strategy prevents fast policy updates from destabilizing high-level semantic representations while still allowing the action expert to learn efficiently from online feedback. Experiments on the CALVIN benchmark and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that TEMPO consistently outperforms both pretrained state-of-the-art VLA models and the RL post-training baseline, while reaching and maintaining higher evaluation rewards on two real-world tasks.

</details>


### [109] [Learning Fault-Tolerant Locomotion with Adaptive Gait Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07328)
*Giovanbattista Gravina,Luca Rossini,Carlo Rizzardo,Arturo Laurenzi,Nikos Tsagarakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度强化学习的容错运动控制方法，用于四足机器人在执行器功率损失下的故障鲁棒运动。采用非对称演员-评论家架构，引入潜在对齐损失和可学习的步态频率参数，使机器人能够自适应地重组步态协调与节律。方法在68kg大型四足机器人的高保真仿真（不平地形）和真实实验（平坦地面）中均得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件故障要求腿式机器人快速重组协调与步态时序以维持稳定和机动性，这对大型四足机器人尤为困难，因为其更大的质量和更紧的驱动极限使得小型平台上常见的高频激进补偿策略不可行。

Method: 提出深度强化学习框架：(1) 采用非对称Actor-Critic架构，评论家在训练时获得特权信息，演员从本体感觉观测中重建对应的潜在表征；(2) 引入潜在对齐损失，促使演员与评论家的表征保持一致；(3) 在动作空间中增加可学习的步态频率参数，实现自适应步态时序调节，无需预设故障腿策略。

Result: 该方法在高保真仿真中验证了不平地形下的容错能力，并在68kg四足机器人的平坦地面真实实验中成功部署，展现出在驱动功率损失情况下维持稳定性和机动性的能力。

Conclusion: 非对称策略学习结合步态频率自适应，可为大型四足机器人提供有效的容错运动控制方案，克服质量与驱动限制带来的补偿策略可行性挑战。

Abstract: Hardware failures require legged robots to rapidly reorganize coordination and gait timing to maintain stability and mobility. This is particularly challenging for larger quadrupeds, where increased mass and tighter actuation limits reduce the feasibility of aggressive, high-frequency compensation strategies often observed on smaller platforms. In this work, we propose a deep reinforcement learning approach for fault-tolerant locomotion under actuator power loss. The method employs an asymmetric actor-critic architecture in which the critic has access to privileged information during training, while the actor learns to reconstruct a corresponding latent representation from proprioceptive observations. We introduce a latent-alignment loss that encourages consistency between actor and critic representations. Additionally, we augment the action space with a learnable gait frequency parameter, enabling adaptive gait timing in response to terrain variations and actuator degradation without predefined faulty-leg strategies. The approach is validated in high-fidelity simulation on uneven terrain and real-world experiments on flat ground using a 68 kg quadruped robot.

</details>


### [110] [Depth-Wise Probing and Pruning of the Planning Token in a Driving Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07361)
*Harisankar Babu,Benjamin Coors,Christopher Lang,Hendrik Berkemeyer,Tamim Asfour,Simon Foell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过将规划token从VLA模型的32个解码器层逐一解码，发现语义意图在第一层即可线性解码（准确率97.7%），而规划轨迹兼容性随深度逐步提升；通过按角度偏差排序可移除8/32层，仅带来约5%的开环误差增加和1.33倍解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型将驾驶决策路由通过深层语言模型，但动作本身需要多少深度尚不清楚。作者希望诊断规划token在不同解码器层中的信息表征状态，以理解深度对动作规划的实际必要性。

Method: 借用生成规划器作为轨迹空间logit透镜，从全部32个解码器层解码规划token，测量两个信号：(1)导航命令的线性可解码性（命令探针准确率）；(2)与冻结原生规划器的轨迹兼容性（开环Avg-L2）。同时通过规划token的角偏差对层排序以实现层剪枝，并评估速度提升与误差变化。

Result: 命令探针在第一层即达97.7%准确率（随机基数为16.7%）；与冻结原生规划器的兼容性随深度逐步改善，开环Avg-L2在最后一层达到最小值2.11m；第一层学习读出可恢复大部分差距；移除8层后开环误差仅增加约5%，获得1.33倍解码器加速；样本量内未统计显著发现家族特异性退化。

Conclusion: 语义意图在早期层就已线性可解码，规划信息在早期已存在但格式未与部署规划器对齐；深度主要服务于信息格式转换而非信息获取，因此可通过合理的层排序移除部分解码器层实现加速而几乎不损失性能。发现局限于ORION检查点和Bench2Drive设置。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models route driving decisions through a deep language model, but it is unclear how much of that depth the action itself requires. We study a representative driving VLA whose entire plan is carried by a single planning token that a generative planner decodes into a trajectory. Borrowing the planner as a trajectory-space logit lens, we decode the planning token from every one of the 32 decoder layers and measure two signals: the linear decodability of the navigation command and trajectory compatibility with the frozen native planner. Our diagnostic shows that semantic intent is linearly decodable early: command-probe accuracy reaches 97.7\% after the first decoder layer, compared with 16.7\% chance. In contrast, compatibility with the frozen native planner improves gradually across depth, with open-loop Avg-L2 reaching its minimum of 2.11\,m only at the final layer. Learned readouts from the first layer recover much of this gap, indicating that planning information is already present early but is not yet represented in the format expected by the deployed planner. Ranking decoder layers by the angular deviation they induce in the planning token permits removal of 8 of 32 layers within an approximately 5\% relative open-loop error increase and yields a measured 1.33$\times$ decoder speedup. At the evaluated sample size, no family-specific degradation is statistically resolved. These findings are limited to the evaluated ORION checkpoint and Bench2Drive setup.

</details>


### [111] [TRACE: Ergodic Trajectory Optimization for Active Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02304)
*Ziyue Zheng,Linli Shi,Bingkun He,Wen Jiang,Ziyun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TRACE方法，将主动重建视为遍历覆盖（ergodic coverage）问题，通过核遍历水平规划器（kernel-ergodic horizon planner）联合优化地图构建与轨迹规划，在Replica数据集上比NBV基线PSNR提升1.5 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯溅射（Gaussian-splatting）的主动重建系统采用贪心方式选择观测，每次只优化单一的最优下一视角（NBV），并用短视距路径规划连接所选视角。这种贪心解耦方式忽略了场景信息的全局结构，产生低效轨迹，浪费了在视角之间移动过程中的感知能力。

Method: 将主动重建形式化为遍历覆盖问题：使传感器轨迹的时间平均空间统计量匹配由当前地图诱导的目标信息分布。目标分布在线由不确定性和可见性推导，并通过核遍历水平规划器（kernel-ergodic horizon planner）结合梯度流（gradient flow）和足迹衰减（footprint depletion）计算遍历轨迹，实现建图与轨迹优化的闭环。

Result: 在Replica数据集上与NBV基线对比，PSNR提升1.5 dB。

Conclusion: 通过全局遍历覆盖策略替代贪心NBV，可以显著提升主动重建的感知效率与重建质量。

Abstract: Existing active reconstruction systems with Gaussian-splatting maps select observations greedily, optimizing a single next-best-view (NBV) at each step and connecting the chosen views by short-horizon path planning. This greedy decoupling disregards the global structure of scene information, producing inefficient trajectories that waste sensing capacity in transit between selected views. In this work, we study active reconstruction as an ergodic coverage problem: the time-averaged spatial statistics of the sensor trajectory should match a target information distribution induced by the current map. Our approach derives this target distribution online from uncertainty and visibility, and calculates ergodic trajectories via a kernel-ergodic horizon planner with gradient flow and footprint depletion, closing the loop between mapping and trajectory optimization. We thoroughly evaluate TRACE on the Replica dataset against the Next-Best-View (NBV) baselines, improving PSNR by 1.5 dB. Code: https://github.com/spikelab-jhu/trace-active-reconstruction.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Towards Multi-Label Graph Foundation Models: from Single-Vector Representation Learning to Multi-Semantic Basis Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06394)
*Dongxiao He,Jiayu Zhang,Jitao Zhao,Yi Wang,Di Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MSB-GFM，一个用于跨领域多标签节点分类的图基础模型框架，通过多语义基元表示学习范式将多标签节点建模为语义基元的自适应组合，并采用语义-结构双通道架构与领域对抗训练实现跨领域知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作存在两方面局限：1) 多标签节点分类方法仅考虑同域场景，跨域泛化能力有限；2) 现有图基础模型（GFMs）基于单标签假设，将多标签节点压缩到单一表示点，导致语义纠缠，难以同时区分多个标签。因此需要一种能同时处理多标签语义和跨领域泛化的模型。

Method: MSB-GFM包含两大核心组件：1) 多语义基元表示学习范式，将每个多标签节点建模为语义基元的自适应组合（加权融合），提供灵活的表示能力以建模多种语义；2) 语义-结构双通道架构结合领域对抗训练，实现跨领域知识的有效迁移，增强模型的跨域泛化能力。

Result: 大量实验证明了该模型在跨领域多标签节点分类任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: MSB-GFM通过多语义基元表示和双通道对抗训练架构，成功解决了现有GFMs在跨领域多标签节点分类中的语义纠缠问题，为图基础模型在多标签场景下的跨域泛化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multi-label node classification is an important yet challenging task in graph learning, where nodes exhibit multiple semantics simultaneously. Existing methods for multi-label node classification can effectively model multiple labels, while only considering in-domain scenarios where the model needs to be trained and tested within the same graph domain, resulting in limited cross-domain generalization. Recently, Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for learning transferable graph representations across diverse graph domains and downstream tasks. However, existing GFMs are built upon single-label assumption, where all nodes are arbitrarily regarded as containing only one class of semantic and embedded into a single representation. For multi-label nodes, such a representation essentially approximates multiple semantics with a single point in the representation space, inevitably leading to semantic entanglement and making simultaneous discrimination of multiple labels difficult. To address these limitations, we propose a Multi-Semantic Basis Graph Foundation Model (MSB-GFM), a framework for cross-domain multi-label node classification. Specifically, we introduce a multi-semantic basis representation learning paradigm that models each multi-label node as an adaptive composition of semantic bases, thereby enabling flexible representational capacity for modeling multiple semantics. Furthermore, we develop a semantic-structure dual-channel architecture with domain adversarial training for effective cross-domain knowledge transfer. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our model.

</details>


### [113] [EntropyMoE: Entropy-Aware Sparse Expert Routing for Tokenizer-Free LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06398)
*Bo Liu,Muxuab Yu,Yu Zhang,Pengfei Gao,Yongping Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EntropyMoE是一种基于熵的Mixture-of-Experts架构，专为字节级大语言模型中的动态字节补丁设计。它用Top-K专家层替代全局补丁Transformer中的稠密前馈模块，以补丁熵作为路由特征，实现了与动态补丁构建一致的稀疏条件计算，在匹配的稠密和稀疏基线上取得了最低的保留bits-per-byte，同时保持相当的下游准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的字节级大语言模型通过将字节动态分组为补丁实现了无分词器的建模，但所有字节补丁仍使用相同的稠密前馈计算，无法根据补丁的语义和粒度差异自适应调整模型容量，导致计算资源的浪费和建模能力的不足。

Method: EntropyMoE将全局补丁Transformer中的稠密前馈模块替换为Top-K专家层。每个动态补丁作为专家路由的基本单元，其字节覆盖范围决定工作量核算；路由器直接从补丁熵中选择专家，补丁熵和长度共同构成调控专家特化的特征空间，从而实现与动态补丁构建逻辑一致的稀疏条件计算。

Result: 在匹配的稠密和稀疏基线上，EntropyMoE取得了最低的保留bits-per-byte，同时在下游任务上保持了与基线相当的性能。

Conclusion: 实验证明补丁熵是稀疏条件计算的有效路由坐标，将MoE建模从基于分词器的表示扩展到字节级无分词器表示，为动态字节补丁场景下的自适应稀疏计算提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent byte-level large language models (LLMs) have made tokenizer-free modeling increasingly competitive by grouping bytes into dynamically sized patches. However, existing byte-patch architectures still apply the same dense feed-forward computation to every patch. This uniform computation cannot adapt model capacity to variations in patch semantics and granularity. We address this limitation with EntropyMoE, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture designed for dynamic byte patches. EntropyMoE replaces the dense feed-forward modules in the global patch Transformer with Top-K expert layers. Each dynamic patch serves as the basic unit of expert routing, and its byte coverage determines its contribution to workload accounting. The router selects experts directly from patch entropy, using the same granularity signal that underlies dynamic patch construction to organize sparse computation. Patch entropy and length jointly define the feature space for regulating expert specialization. Experiments show that EntropyMoE achieves the lowest held-out bits-per-byte among matched dense and sparse baselines while maintaining comparable downstream accuracy. These results establish patch entropy as an effective routing coordinate for sparse conditional computation and extend Mixture-of-Experts modeling beyond tokenizer-based representations.

</details>


### [114] [Beyond Routing Weights: Faithful Response-Level Interpretation of Mixture-of-Experts Reward Models via Contribution Contrast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06400)
*Yifan Wang,Jinyi Mu,Mayank Jobanputra,Yu Wang,Soyoung Oh,Isabel Valera,Vera Demberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出贡献对比（CoCo）方法，用于解释稀疏混合专家（MoE）奖励模型的专家行为。与仅基于路由权重的解释方法不同，CoCo通过选择具有最大贡献差异的chosen-rejected响应对来联合捕捉路由和偏好行为，从而更全面地表征专家角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于稀疏MoE奖励模型的解释方法依赖路由权重，但路由权重只能揭示专家"接收"哪些提示，无法揭示专家如何"判断"响应，不能全面解释专家行为。这促使作者提出更全面的解释方法。

Method: CoCo方法通过选择chosen-rejected响应对中具有最大贡献差异的样本，联合捕获路由行为和偏好判断行为，从而对专家角色进行响应级解释。这是首个系统研究MoE奖励模型解释方法的工作。

Result: 在自动评估和人工评估中，CoCo相比基于路由器、基于分数和基于稀疏自编码器的替代方法，产生了更连贯、更忠实、更专业化的专家解释，同时保持了有竞争力的奖励建模精度。

Conclusion: CoCo是解释MoE奖励模型中专家行为的有效方法，填补了路由权重解释的不足，并在多个评估维度上优于现有替代方案。

Abstract: Reward models are central to learning from human preferences, yet identifying what drives their predictions remains challenging. Recent sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) reward models seek to improve interpretability by routing prompts to specialized experts and characterizing experts through examples with high routing weights. However, routing weights only reveal which prompts an expert $\textit{receives}$, not how it $\textit{judges}$ responses, providing only a partial account of expert behavior. We therefore propose $\textbf{Co}$ntribution-$\textbf{Co}$ntrast ($\textbf{CoCo}$) response-level interpretation, which faithfully characterizes experts' roles using chosen-rejected response pairs with the largest contribution contrasts, jointly capturing routing and preference behavior. Across automatic and human evaluations, CoCo yields more coherent, faithful, and specialized interpretations than router-based, score-based, and sparse autoencoder-based alternatives while maintaining competitive reward modeling accuracy. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic study of interpretation methods for MoE reward models.

</details>


### [115] [Interpretable Unsupervised Community Detection with LLM-Symbolized Structured Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06402)
*Aoting Zeng,Kai Wang,Jianwei Wang,Yuxiang Sun,Yizhang He,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LUCID是一种由LLM引导的、可解释的、无需训练的无监督社区检测方法，受相变动力学启发，通过四阶段流水线（局部初始化、多因子合并、多粒度细化、全局选择）利用LLM归纳形式化规则，将隐式知识转化为显式的可解释逻辑结构，在真实数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于目标函数的社区检测方法难以处理复杂图结构，深度学习方法提升性能却牺牲可解释性且依赖标注数据和训练过程。LLM具备强大推理能力和世界知识，有望实现可解释且无标签依赖的社区检测。

Method: LUCID设计为四阶段流水线：(1) 局部视角社区初始化——利用k-ego上下文和无监督节点角色编码局部图结构；(2) 多因子社区合并——使用LLM归纳的规则迭代合并局部社区；(3) 多粒度社区细化——并行应用LLM归纳的粗细粒度规则以减少边界噪声；(4) 全局视角社区选择——基于拓扑紧密度和边界清晰度筛选高质量社区。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，LUCID作为无监督方法取得了最先进的性能，并一致优于领先的无监督和半监督基线方法。

Conclusion: LUCID验证了LLM引导下的无监督社区检测在性能和可解释性上均具优势，为图分析领域提供了一种无需标注数据、无需训练且可解释的社区发现新范式。

Abstract: Community detection is a fundamental task in graph analytics that aims to identify cohesive groups of entities with similar behaviors or interests. Classic objective-driven methods struggle with complex graph structures, while deep-learning approaches improve performance at the expense of interpretability and rely on labeled data and training. Large language models (LLMs), with strong reasoning capabilities and world knowledge, are promising for interpretable, label-free community detection. To leverage these strengths, we propose LUCID, an LLM-guided, interpretable, training-free, and unsupervised community detection method. Inspired by phase-transition kinetics in natural systems, where complex structures emerge through initialization, merging, refinement, and selection, LUCID is designed as a four-stage pipeline. Within this pipeline, the LLM induces formal rules that translate implicit knowledge into explicit and interpretable logical structures. Specifically, (1) the Local-View Community Initialization stage encodes local graph structures using k-ego contexts and unsupervised node roles; (2) the Multi-factor Community Merge stage uses LLM-induced rules to iteratively merge local communities; (3) the Multi-grain Community Refinement stage applies LLM-induced coarse-to-fine rules in parallel to reduce boundary noise; and (4) the Global-view Community Selection stage identifies high-quality communities based on topological compactness and boundary clarity. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that LUCID, as an unsupervised approach, achieves state-of-the-art performance and consistently outperforms leading unsupervised and semi-supervised baselines.

</details>


### [116] [ADIAS: Automated Design of Interactive Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06410)
*Lekang Jiang,Bohan Tang,Stephan Goetz,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出以议题为中心的智能体优化方法（ADIAS），通过维护持久化议题状态（包括议题身份、生命周期、证据及干预-结果历史）来指导全代码智能体设计的迭代修复，相比现有以候选为中心的优化方法在五个交互基准上平均提升25.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动智能体设计方法是以候选为中心组织跨轮次经验，导致修复进度隐式化，存在修复目标不精准、部分进展整合缓慢、无效干预跨轮传播等问题。

Method: 提出议题中心智能体优化范式，将修复进展作为显式的持久化议题状态携带，而非每轮从候选历史中重新推导。并实例化为ADIAS框架，包含两个机制：持久化议题状态（维持稳定的议题身份、生命周期状态、支持证据和干预-结果历史）和议题引导优化（利用该状态联合提议修复目标和修订方向，进行聚焦的全代码修改）。

Result: 在五个交互基准测试中，ADIAS相比最强基线平均提升25.2%，在四种骨干模型上均有稳定提升。消融实验表明移除持久化议题状态或用候选中心策略替代议题中心修订会导致性能下降高达40.7%。

Conclusion: 以议题为中心的优化框架能有效提升自动智能体设计的修复效率和性能，显式追踪议题生命周期相较于候选中心范式具有显著优势。

Abstract: Automated agent design improves agent harnesses through iterative revision, evaluation, and feedback summarization. Existing methods are largely candidate-centric: cross-round experience is organized around candidate agents, which leaves the repair progress implicit. This causes inefficient repair targeting, slow consolidation of partial progress, and propagation of ineffective interventions across rounds. Therefore, we formulate issue-centric agent optimization, in which repair progress is carried forward as an explicit persistent issue state to guide optimization, rather than re-derived from candidate history in each round. We instantiate the formulation in ADIAS, a framework for automated full-code agent design with two mechanisms. A persistent issue state maintains stable issue identities, lifecycle status, supporting evidence, and intervention-outcome histories. Issue-guided optimization uses this state to jointly propose repair targets and revision directions for subsequent focused full-code modification. Across five interactive benchmarks, ADIAS outperforms the strongest baseline by 25.2% on average and achieves consistent gains across four backbone models. Controlled ablations further show that removing persistent issue state or replacing issue-centric revision with candidate-centric policies leads to performance drops of up to 40.7%.

</details>


### [117] [Learning to Predict Middle-Layer Attention in MLLMs for Visual Token Prunin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06411)
*Yuyao Sun,Tao Deng,Shuang Li,Deqing Wang,Hao Geng,Minjun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MAP方法，通过预测中间层注意力来在进入语言模型层之前剪枝视觉token，在仅保留5.56%视觉token的情况下保持97.5%性能，实现3.09倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）因处理大量视觉token导致计算成本高昂。现有视觉token剪枝方法依赖固定中间层的注意力指导，但该层对问题的响应度在不同样本间差异显著，固定层不最优；此外，获取中间层注意力需先处理大量视觉token经过多个语言模型层，此时大量计算已消耗。

Method: 提出Middle-layer Attention Prediction (MAP)方法，包含两个关键组件：(1) Question Contrastive Teacher Selection，通过对比原始问题与参考问题下的注意力来识别样本特异的教师层；(2) 将选定层的注意力蒸馏到一个轻量级预测器中，该预测器从多模态输入特征估计视觉token重要性。推理时，MAP结合预测的重要性分数与多样性准则，在第一个语言模型层之前完成视觉token剪枝，无需注意力图，兼容现有推理加速技术。

Result: 在LLaVA-NeXT-7B上跨越十个基准测试，MAP仅保留5.56%的视觉token就保持了未剪枝模型97.5%的性能，实现3.09倍端到端加速。

Conclusion: MAP通过样本自适应的教师层选择和轻量级注意力预测，在进入语言模型层前即可完成高效视觉token剪枝，既解决了固定层次优问题，又避免了先处理大量token的计算浪费，在性能与效率之间取得了优异平衡。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance across diverse vision-language tasks, but their efficiency is limited by the cost of processing numerous visual tokens. Visual token pruning can reduce this cost, but requires accurate token importance estimates. Recent studies have demonstrated that text-to-vision attention from middle language model layers can effectively guide visual token pruning, typically using attention from a predefined middle layer to select the visual tokens to retain. Two problems therefore remain. First, our analysis shows that the layer whose attention is most responsive to the question varies substantially across samples, making a fixed layer suboptimal. Second, obtaining attention from the appropriate middle layer requires processing numerous visual tokens through several language model layers, by which point considerable computation has already been spent. To address both problems, we propose Middle-layer Attention Prediction (MAP), which uses Question Contrastive Teacher Selection to identify a sample-specific teacher layer by contrasting attention under the original and reference questions, and distills attention from the selected layer into a lightweight predictor that estimates visual token importance from multi-modal input features. During inference, MAP combines the predicted importance scores with a diversity criterion to prune visual tokens before the first language model layer. Thus, MAP requires no attention maps for pruning and remains compatible with existing inference acceleration techniques. Across ten benchmarks on LLaVA-NeXT-7B, MAP retains 97.5% of the unpruned model performance with only 5.56% of the visual tokens, yielding a 3.09x end-to-end speedup.

</details>


### [118] [WebGrader: Training LLMs for Web Development with Self-Evolving Programmatic Grader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06474)
*Boshui Chen,Huiping Liu,Shaolei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WebGrader是一个自进化的程序化评分器，用于大型语言模型生成的网站的功能性评估与强化学习训练。它从网站请求自动推导交互流程，以可执行Flow Contract表示，在真实浏览器中执行并收集多模态证据，通过残差驱动的离线循环发现可复用验证技能并冻结。在WebGen-Bench上训练的8B策略达到52.01%功能成功率，超越多种强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型从自然语言描述生成完整网站的能力逐步提升，强化学习成为弥合功能差距的核心方法，但该训练范式受限于奖励设计。手工编写的浏览器脚本虽然可执行但成本高昂，无法应对开放式需求；而VLM和GUI-agent评分器虽可扩展，但可能在关键状态出现前就发布判定结果，导致奖励信号不准确。

Method: WebGrader采用四阶段分离架构：(1) 测试规划——从网站请求自动推导所需交互流，每条流表示为可执行的Flow Contract；(2) 动作锚定——在真实浏览器中实例化项目，将目标动作锚定到源代码和实时DOM；(3) 证据收集——沿同一浏览器轨迹采集视觉、DOM、响应和持久化状态证据；(4) 语义判定——仅在观察到请求的状态转换后才发出Pass判定。此外，通过残差驱动的离线循环发现可复用的验证器技能，在不相交的验证页面上筛选，并在策略训练前冻结已晋升的技能图。

Result: 在WebGen-Bench基准上，WebGrader训练的8B策略达到52.01%的功能成功率，比匹配的"外观加脚本"奖励高出7.88个百分点，并超越o4-mini和DeepSeek-v4-flash。在WG-core-250上，策略达到44.953的满分，超越Qwen3-Coder-480B。

Conclusion: WebGrader通过将测试规划、动作锚定、证据收集与语义判定四阶段分离，实现对网站功能请求待观察状态的可靠判定，有效解决了强化学习训练中奖励设计瓶颈，显著提升了小规模策略模型的功能生成能力，超越包括更大模型在内的多个强基线。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate complete websites from natural-language descriptions, and reinforcement learning has become a central approach to closing their remaining functional gap. This training regime is bottlenecked by reward design. Hand-authored browser scripts are executable yet costly to write for open-ended requirements, while VLM and GUI-agent graders scale but may issue verdicts before observing the decisive state. We propose WebGrader, a self-evolving programmatic grader that autonomously derives the required interaction flows from each website request, represents each flow as an executable Flow Contract, and uses its execution outcome as an RL reward. WebGrader materializes the generated project in a live browser, grounds target actions against the source code and live DOM, and collects visual, DOM, response, and persistent-state evidence along the same browser trajectory. A residual-driven offline loop then discovers reusable verifier skills, screens them on disjoint validation pages, and freezes the promoted skill graph before policy training. By separating test planning, action grounding, evidence collection, and semantic judgment, WebGrader issues a Pass verdict only after observing the requested transition. On WebGen-Bench, WebGrader trains an 8B policy to a 52.01% functional success rate, outperforming a matched appearance-plus-script reward by 7.88 points and surpassing o4-mini and DeepSeek-v4-flash. On WG-core-250, the policy reaches a Full Score of 44.953 and surpasses Qwen3-Coder-480B.

</details>


### [119] [Can MLLMs Decode the Creative Leap? Introducing C4 for Cross-Concept Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06501)
*Ming Wang,Yuqing Zhang,Tingna Xie,Xiangju Li,Xiaocui Yang,Daling Wang,Shi Feng,Yifei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出C4框架，一个受认知启发的基于成语的跨概念创造力评估框架，包含184个合成项和37个人工创建的跨概念成语图形，在五种任务设置下对10个MLLM进行评估，最强闭源模型准确率仅约50%，开源模型更低，揭示了当前MLLM在跨概念创造性解码方面的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的创造力在设计、沟通、教育和人机协作中至关重要，但现有评估因缺乏显式目标和奖励信号而困难。跨概念理解是接受性创造力的核心认知能力，但缺乏系统性的评估方法。作者旨在通过成语这一文化密集且自然体现跨概念关系的载体，构建一个结构明确、可批量生成且有精确答案的创造力评估框架。

Method: 将项目构建操作化为跨概念编码，模型推理操作化为跨概念解码。构建C4框架：编码组件通过手动标注和第三方审核的跨概念网络中的桥接路径，将目标槽映射到可成像的替代概念，以桥接数量和深度作为难度指标并生成精确答案。基于此构建C4-Eval数据集（184个合成项+37个人工成语图形），每个项目在五种任务设置下实例化，共884个主答案恢复案例。

Result: 在10个评估的MLLM中，最强闭源模型的主要准确率为50.7%和48.0%，开源模型显著较低。候选约束能大幅提高准确率，但桥接提示和解释请求仅带来适度提升。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM通过跨概念关系解码创造性编码含义的能力存在实质性差距。该框架提供了一种可复现的、具有明确结构、难度指标和精确答案的创造力评估方法，代码已开源。

Abstract: Creative capabilities of MLLMs matter in design, communication, education, and human--AI collaboration, yet remain difficult to evaluate because explicit targets and reward signals are scarce compared with accuracy-oriented tasks. Cross-concept understanding is a core cognitive capacity underlying receptive creativity. It enables a perceiver to recover intended meaning from non-obvious but meaningful conceptual relations. We operationalize item construction as cross-concept encoding and model inference as cross-concept decoding. We introduce C4, a cognition-inspired evaluation framework for Chengyu (Chinese idiom)-based Cross-Concept Creativity. Its encoding component maps target slots to imageable substitute concepts along bridge paths in a manually annotated and third-party-reviewed cross-concept network, enabling batch generation with explicit structure, difficulty indexed by bridge count and depth, and exact answers. Using this framework, we instantiate the C4 Evaluation Set (C4-Eval), comprising 184 synthetic items and 37 human-created cross-concept chengyu figures collected from online sources. We manually construct and review cross-concept relations, bridge paths, and reasoning processes for the collected figures. Each C4-Eval item is instantiated in five task settings, yielding 884 primary answer-recovery cases. Across ten evaluated MLLMs, the strongest closed models reach 50.7% and 48.0% primary accuracy, while open-source models remain substantially lower. Candidate constraints improve accuracy sharply, but bridge hints and explanation requests provide only modest gains. These results expose a substantial gap in how current MLLMs decode creatively encoded meaning through cross-concept relations. The code is in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [120] [KNOWPLAN: Knowledge-Driven AI Agents for Smart Degree Pathway Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06530)
*Shuheng Cao,Weijia Zhang,Jiaqi Wu,Xiyun Hu,Yat Yang,Juqy Chen,Zhaoxiang Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KnowPlan是一个两阶段解耦的课程规划系统：CatalogBrowse负责从异构大学来源（目录、网页、JSON、PDF）抽取课程数据，DegreeMap负责在抽取结果上做个性化的学位规划优化。两者通过明确的文档契约接口衔接，并通过CP-SAT求解器进行词典序优化。在100所大学的广泛轨和6所学校的密集轨上，CatalogBrowse达到96.2%的库存召回率和88.7%的掩码源恢复率（比穷举爬虫减少47%的源访问），DegreeMap保持100%硬可行性并将个性化效用提升+0.066，全流水线以0.015的效用差距认证99.5%的请求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学位规划系统将课程目录重建和学生路径优化耦合在一起，导致抽取错误与规划错误互相掩盖——因为驱动自身爬取的规划器永远不会学到当前计划不需要的事实。作者主张将两阶段严格分离，并度量阶段间接口而非假设其正确，以消除失败模式的相互隐藏。

Method: 系统由两个严格解耦的阶段组成：(1) CatalogBrowse，仅探索来源、不使用任何用户画像，通过有限原子目录义务的低置信期望边际增益评分合法操作，经平台适配器确定性解析，辅以跨度约束的从句到AST模型作为回退，以索引、模式、溯源和引用完整性的闭包证书终止而非奖励阈值，输出三个带溯源链接的JSON文档作为契约；(2) DegreeMap，仅消费这些文档，编译为带类型的需求超图，使用CP-SAT在硬可行性、完成时限、负荷与风险、个性化效用和期权价值上进行词典序优化，使每阶段在前一阶段已证最优的函数空间内优化，并保持在求解器预算内可认证。

Result: 在100所大学的广泛轨和6所学校的密集轨评测中：CatalogBrowse达到96.2%库存召回率和88.7%掩码源恢复率，同时比穷举爬虫减少47%的源访问量；DegreeMap保持100.0%硬可行性，个性化效用比最强基线高+0.066；完整流水线以0.015的效用差距（相对于特权金标准图）认证99.5%的请求。

Conclusion: KnowPlan验证了抽取优先（extraction-first）的架构边界在真实规模和异构数据条件下的有效性。通过将课程重建与规划优化彻底解耦，并以形式化的文档契约和闭包证书定义阶段间接口，系统在保持高可认证性和硬可行性的同时大幅提升了效率与个性化效用，证明了"先抽取、后规划"范式相对于耦合设计的优势。

Abstract: Planning a degree from official university sources requires solving two problems in order. The institution's curriculum must first be reconstructed from catalogs, departmental pages, JSON endpoints, and PDFs that share no schema, and only then can a student-specific path be optimized under prerequisite logic and overlapping requirement constraints. Coupling the two lets each failure mode hide the other, because a planner that drives its own crawling never learns facts its current plan does not need. We present KnowPlan, which enforces an extraction-first boundary and measures the interface between the stages rather than assuming it. CatalogBrowse explores with no access to any user profile. It scores legal actions by lower-confidence expected marginal gain over a finite set of atomic catalog obligations per unit of source access, parses deterministically through platform adapters with a span-constrained clause-to-AST model fallback, and terminates on a closure certificate over index, schema, provenance, and reference completeness instead of a reward threshold. Its output contract is three provenance-linked JSON documents. DegreeMap consumes only those documents. It compiles them into a typed requirement hypergraph and optimizes lexicographically with CP-SAT over hard feasibility, completion horizon, load and risk, personalized utility, and option value, so that each stage optimizes inside the previous stage's proven optimum and stays certifiable within the solver budget. Across a 100-university broad track and a six-school dense track, CatalogBrowse reaches 96.2% inventory recall and 88.7% masked-source recovery at 47% less source access than an exhaustive crawler, DegreeMap holds 100.0% hard feasibility while improving personalized utility by +0.066 over the strongest baseline, and the full pipeline certifies 99.5% of requests with a utility gap to the privileged gold graph of 0.015.

</details>


### [121] [TaskSense: Focusing on What Matters in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06544)
*SM Mazharul Islam,Manfred Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TaskSense框架，通过可微随机空间注意力机制和辅助逆动力学目标，使世界模型在视觉控制任务中只关注并重建任务相关区域，从而在视觉干扰环境下显著优于DreamerV3基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型通过重建完整观测来学习潜状态，隐式地迫使表征保留整个视觉输入的信息。然而任务相关内容通常只占观测的一小部分，背景杂波和干扰物消耗了宝贵表征容量，导致潜表征偏向建模与任务无关的视觉内容，稀释了控制相关特征的学习信号，在视觉干扰环境下严重损害下游性能。

Method: TaskSense框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 可微随机空间注意力机制，以先前潜状态为条件，在潜编码前强制执行任务相关性；(2) 辅助逆动力学训练目标，引导注意力聚焦于控制相关区域；(3) 仅重建被注意区域而非完整观测，同时解码器以采样到的注意力图为条件，实现随机注意力下的一致性重建。

Result: 在DeepMind Control Suite上TaskSense与DreamerV3基线性能相当，在Distracting Control Suite上持续优于DreamerV3，展现出对视觉干扰显著增强的鲁棒性。定性分析进一步证实，受逆动力学监督引导的注意力能够一致地定位控制相关区域并抑制无关视觉内容。

Conclusion: 任务中心的世界建模方法有效解决了视觉重建目标与控制目标之间的不匹配问题，通过注意力引导的稀疏重建提升了视觉控制在干扰环境下的鲁棒性，验证了"先筛选任务相关区域再编码"范式对世界模型表征学习的价值。

Abstract: World models for visual control typically learn compact latent states by reconstructing observations, implicitly encouraging representations to preserve information across the entire visual input. However, task-relevant content often occupies only a small fraction of the observation, while background clutter and distractors consume valuable representational capacity. This mismatch between visual reconstruction and control objectives biases latent representations to model task-irrelevant visual content, diluting learning signals for control-relevant features and severely degrading downstream performance under visual distractions. We introduce TaskSense, a task-centric world modeling framework that enforces task relevance before latent encoding through a differentiable stochastic spatial attention mechanism conditioned on the previous latent state. To steer attention toward control-relevant regions, we augment training with an auxiliary inverse-dynamics objective. Rather than reconstructing the full observation, the world model reconstructs only the attended regions, encouraging latent representations to preserve task-relevant information while discarding irrelevant visual content. The decoder is further conditioned on the sampled attention map, enabling consistent reconstruction despite stochastic attention. Compared with the DreamerV3 baseline, TaskSense maintains competitive performance on the DeepMind Control Suite while consistently outperforming DreamerV3 on the Distracting Control Suite, demonstrating substantially improved robustness to visual distractions. Qualitative analysis further confirms that the learned attention, guided by inverse-dynamics supervision, consistently localizes control-relevant regions while suppressing irrelevant visual content.

</details>


### [122] [Divergent Response Modes in Frontier Language Models Under Steering Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06578)
*Ali Jalal-Kamali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究对来自六个开发者的六个前沿模型进行了行为可调控性（steerability）的系统评估，使用300对基线与引导提示词覆盖三个类别（价值观冲突、推理激发、推理抑制）。研究发现模型之间不仅在调控程度上存在差异，更在响应模式上存在质的不同，例如GPT-5会拒绝披露推理过程但保留答案（99% vs 其他模型0%）。通过基于Llama的线性探针实验，研究者以0.87的留出准确率从残差流解码行为，并通过干预将行为从0%驱动到86%。所有发现在token预算补救和假设盲判断提示的对照实验中均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型使用不同的数据、目标和安全流程进行训练，但令人惊讶的是，这些差异是否会在明确的定向引导压力下产生可度量的行为差异，仍未被充分探索。理解不同模型在显式调控下的行为异同对于模型评估、安全审计和部署选型至关重要。

Method: 使用来自六个开发者的六个前沿模型，构建300对配对的基线与引导项，覆盖三类行为调控任务（价值观冲突、推理激发、推理抑制）加上40个验证项。所有六个模型作为盲法同行评审者，根据固定行为标准对每个响应进行分类，产生24,480个判断，并通过留一法共识进行评分。此外，对Llama开放权重模型进行内部机制分析，包括线性探针解码和残差方向注入干预。

Result: 模型不仅在调控偏移量上不同，更在响应模式（mode）的类型上存在质的差异，某些响应模式仅出现在一个或两个模型中。GPT-5以99%的比例拒绝披露推理过程但保留答案，而其他模型均为0%。Claude Opus 4.7和GPT-5以不同的方式抵抗显式抑制指令。Llama的线性探针以0.87留出准确率解码行为，注入该方向可将行为从0%驱动到86%。所有发现均通过token预算补救和假设盲判断控制实验验证。

Conclusion: 前沿语言模型之间的可控性差异不仅是程度上的，更是本质模式上的——不同模型的训练管线产生了可度量的、可内部归因的行为差异。这类行为指纹为模型理解、安全评估和可解释性研究提供了新的视角与方法框架。

Abstract: Frontier language models are trained using distinct data, objectives, and safety pipelines. Whether these differences produce measurably different behaviors under explicit steering pressure remains underexplored. This study evaluates behavioral steerability across six frontier models from six developers using 300 paired base and steered items over three categories: values-conflict, reasoning-elicitation, and reasoning-suppression (plus 40 validation items). All six models act as blind peer judges and classify every response based on fixed behavioral rubrics. The resulting 24,480 judgments are scored by leave-one-out consensus. We find that models differ not just in how much steering shifts their behavior but in what kind (mode) of response they give, and some response modes appear in only one or two of them. GPT-5 deflects requests to disclose its reasoning while leaving its answer intact (99% vs. 0% for all other models). Claude Opus 4.7 and GPT-5 resist explicit suppression instructions and in different ways. Using Llama as the open-weight model, we trace the largest behavioral split to its internals. A linear probe decodes the behavior from the residual stream at 0.87 held-out accuracy while injecting that direction during generation drives the behavior from 0% to 86% across an intervention sweep. Every finding holds under both a token-budget remediation and a control experiment with a hypothesis-blind judgment prompt.

</details>


### [123] [Automated item evaluation: Predicting item acceptance and rejection using LLM-generated critiques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06609)
*Hotaka Maeda,Yikai Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用大型标准化测试项目中52,759道ELA和数学题目的历史拒绝数据，微调DeBERTaV3-large分类器构建了基于题目文本的自动化试题评估（AIE）模型，结合题目原文与Qwen3生成的题目评论的融合模型取得了最佳性能（Accuracy=.75, F1=.64, AUC=.80），其中数学预测精度远高于ELA，但模型在识别偏见、敏感性、公平性相关问题上表现欠佳，强调了人工审查对于公平性关注题目的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的题目质量评估依赖人工专家审查和实地测试，成本高且效率低。作者旨在构建一个近乎全面的自动化试题评估模型，仅基于题目文本即可预测题目被接受或被拒绝的命运，从而减轻人工审查和实地测试的负担。

Method: 使用来自大型标准化测试项目的52,759道ELA和数学题目（34%被永久拒绝），微调DeBERTaV3-large分类器处理原始题目文本；微调第二个DeBERTa分类器处理Qwen3生成的题目评论；再构建融合模型整合两者表征。实验还包括阈值调整（从.5降至.25）以探索不同应用场景下的分类性能。

Result: 融合模型性能最佳（Accuracy=.75, F1=.64, AUC=.80, Sensitivity=.64, Specificity=.81）。数学预测（F1=.73, AUC=.86）显著优于ELA（F1=.51, AUC=.72）。阈值降至.25时，ELA和数学的平均敏感度升至.88和.91，但特异度降至.31和.56。加入题目评论提升了多数拒绝原因下的预测性能。模型对更难题目赋予更高拒绝概率，但难以识别偏见、敏感性、公平性或可及性问题，尤其是ELA题目。

Conclusion: 基于文本的AIE在部分领域是可行的，可作为减少人工审查和实地测试负担的实用工具；但在公平性相关问题上的识别能力不足，凸显了人工审查对于涉及公平性顾虑题目的不可替代性。此外，在自动生成题目成本低于评估成本的场景下，可通过降低决策阈值来优先提升敏感度。

Abstract: Automated item evaluation (AIE) refers to the use of computational methods to assess item quality without requiring manual expert review or field testing of the items under evaluation. We aimed to build a near-comprehensive AIE model by predicting item acceptance and rejection from item text using historical rejection data from a large-scale standardized testing program. The dataset contained 52,759 English language arts (ELA) and mathematics items with 34% permanently rejected from future operational use. Rejection reasons included poor psychometric properties, content issues, bias and sensitivity concerns, and non-content issues. We fine-tuned a DeBERTaV3-large classifier on raw item text, a second DeBERTa classifier on Qwen3-generated item critiques, and a fusion model combining representations from both. The fusion model achieved the strongest overall performance (Accuracy = .75, F1 = .64, AUC = .80, Sensitivity = .64, Specificity = .81). Prediction for math (F1 = .73, AUC = .86) was considerably more accurate than ELA (F1 = .51, AUC = .72). Lowering the decision threshold from .5 to .25 raised average sensitivity for ELA and math to .88 and .91, while reducing specificity to .31 and .56, respectively, which may be preferable in automated item generation contexts where generating items is cheaper than evaluating them. Incorporating item critiques alongside raw item text improved performance across most rejection reasons. The model assigned higher rejection probabilities to more difficult items. However, the fusion model struggled to identify items flagged for bias, sensitivity, fairness, or accessibility, especially for ELA. These findings suggest that text-based AIE is feasible in some areas and may offer a practical tool for reducing the burden of manual review and field testing, while also underscoring the importance of human review for items with fairness concerns.

</details>


### [124] [NxN E-valuation: Hypothesis Certification via a Conformal CRT Null](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06621)
*Bin Wang,Yan Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出NxN E-valuation方法，一种基于e-value的假设认证算法，利用大规模数据集使不同样本互为零假设，直接实现条件随机化检验（CRT），可替代LLM循环验证和留出数据测试，尤其适合LLM探索系统中的假设验证场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在假设提出方面表现出色但存在严重的幻觉问题，直接采信LLM输出不可靠。现有解决方案存在缺陷：循环验证（LLM自我验证或纠正）缺乏独立性和客观性；留出数据测试可能因虚假相关性导致错误假设通过。需要一种无须构建特定认证程序的普适性假设验证方法。

Method: 利用LLM探索系统中天然存在的大规模训练数据集，让不同的样本互相充当彼此的零假设，从而实现一种直接的假设认证机制。该设计具体实现了条件随机化检验（CRT），对每个假设进行统计认证，整个过程无需为每个假设构建专门的零假设或定制认证流程。

Result: 在数据量足够大的前提下，NxN E-valuation可以作为LLM循环验证和留出数据测试的通用更优替代方案，前提是LLM生成的是适用于每个独立样本的假设。

Conclusion: NxN E-valuation提供了一种无需定制化认证程序、基于e-value统计框架的假设验证方案，有效缓解LLM幻觉带来的验证难题，为LLM探索系统提供了更可靠的基础设施。

Abstract: We propose NxN E-valuation, a handy, e-value-based hypothesis-certification algorithm that lets a hypothesis be verified without building any case-specific certification procedure---such as constructing a dedicated null hypothesis---as long as a large enough dataset is available. The method is especially suited to LLM-based exploration systems, where LLMs are remarkably good at proposing hypotheses but suffer badly from hallucination; this hallucination prevents us from harvesting LLM outputs directly, and existing remedies each fall short. The most common solutions include letting the LLM verify or correct itself circular verification and held-out testing (where false hypotheses can still pass via spurious correlations), among other remedies detailed in the introduction. To resolve this, NxN E-valuation exploits the naturally existing large training set and lets different samples serve as null hypotheses for one another. This design directly realizes a conditional randomization test (CRT) that certifies each hypothesis. The approach can be a universally better replacement for at least LLM circular verification and held-out-data testing, provided the LLM's generations are hypotheses that apply to each individual sample.

</details>


### [125] [Shape Your Feed: An LLM-based Agentic System for Conversational Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06632)
*Ziyun Xu,Bosen Ding,Yue Zhang,Ji Qi,Qingyuan Song,Jizhou Huang,Liwei Wang,Jefferey Santelli,Yue Weng,Qichao Que,Zhenheng Yang,Junfeng Pan,Linhong Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Shape Your Feed (SYF)，一个基于LLM的智能体推荐框架，通过三层架构（感知流、服务流、自进化流）实现用户实时、多模态的内容共创式推荐，离线对齐评分模块达到98.85%准确率，在线A/B实验表明能显著提升feed相关性和用户满意度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统普遍采用被动排序范式，仅从隐式行为信号（点击、停留时长）推断用户偏好，而非显式的自然语言输入。这导致用户明确兴趣与被动行为算法交付内容之间存在持续偏差，用户难以表达细微偏好或实时引导信息流。为此亟需一种能弥合推荐优化目标与用户表达意图之间差距的新框架。

Method: SYF采用三层架构：(i)感知流（Perception Flow），从文本提示、语音命令和UI交互中捕获细粒度用户意图；(ii)服务流（Serving Flow），基于持久化的语义画像（Semantic Profile）进行实时智能体重排序和候选物品剪枝；(iii)自进化流（Self-Evolution Flow），通过直接偏好优化（DPO）和LLM-as-a-Judge集成使系统行为与人类判断对齐。

Result: 离线评估表明，SYF的对齐评分模块达到98.85%的准确率，显著优于强少样本基线。大规模在线A/B实验在生产流量上验证了SYF能提升feed相关性和用户情感/满意度，证明其在工业场景下具有实用性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: SYF为工业推荐系统提供了从被动范式转向交互式、用户可引导推荐的实用且可扩展路径，通过LLM驱动的智能体协作与持续自进化机制，实现了对用户实时意图的响应和个性化内容共创。

Abstract: Industrial recommendation systems predominantly adopt a passive ranking paradigm that infers user preferences from implicit behavioral signals (e.g., clicks, dwell time) rather than explicit, natural language inputs. As a result, users experience a persistent discrepancy between their explicit interests and what passive behavioral algorithms deliver, limiting their ability to express nuanced preferences or steer their feed in real time. To address this growing gap between how recommendations are optimized and how users wish to articulate their interests, we present Shape Your Feed (SYF), an LLM-based agentic recommendation framework that enables real-time, multimodal co-curation of content. SYF employs a three-tier architecture: (i) a Perception Flow that captures fine-grained user intent from text prompts, voice commands, and UI interactions; (ii) a Serving Flow that performs real-time agentic re-ranking and pruning of candidate items, grounded in a persistent Semantic Profile encoding evolving user preferences; and (iii) a Self-Evolution Flow that aligns system behavior with human judgments via Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and an LLM-as-a-Judge ensemble. Offline evaluations show that SYF's alignment scoring module achieves 98.85% accuracy, substantially improving over strong few-shot baselines. Large-scale online A/B experiments on production traffic further demonstrate that SYF improves feed relevance and user sentiment, indicating a practical and scalable path toward interactive, user-steerable recommendation in industrial settings.

</details>


### [126] [TRACE: A Multi-Layer Benchmark for Human AI Controller Coordination Under Drift and Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06657)
*Joshua Zuniga,Srinivasan Subramanian,Ramya Madhuri Narapureddy,Md Abdullah Al Hafiz Khan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个用于跨层漂移诊断的基准数据集，基于ALFRED指令追踪注入受控漂移，生成1,918条带五层标注的漂移轨迹，并配以去泄漏协议和跨三类模型家族的基线研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代网络-物理及AI辅助系统将人类、AI决策模块和自动化控制器耦合在同一控制回路中，可信性取决于整个回路而非单一模型。然而现有基准无法捕获跨层的、时间对齐的多层踪迹来诊断漂移的传播路径、原因与恢复方法，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 从ALFRED（日常家务任务的接地指令基准）派生痕迹，注入受控漂移，生成1,918条带漂移的轨迹。每条轨迹是跨五个执行层（状态、观测、决策、规则、控制）的时间对齐逐步记录，标注漂移类型、受影响层、起始时间、责任主体和因果机制，并由独立评分者验证一致性。同时设计泄漏感知协议消除接近完美的起始时间泄漏，并在经典、循环和注意力三类模型家族上进行基线评估。

Result: 在诚实协议下，所有模型家族的漂移识别与归因均显著高于随机和多数类基线（受影响层macro-F1约0.70，责任主体约0.85，因果机制约0.49）。重型注意力模型在该符号基准上相较简单模型无优势。

Conclusion: 该基准及协议为跨层漂移的可诊断性和可归因性提供了首个标准化评测框架，同时揭示了在此类符号化多模态基准任务上模型复杂度并非必要因素。

Abstract: Modern cyber-physical and AI-assisted systems couple human operators, AI decision modules, and automated controllers in a single control loop, so trustworthiness depends on the whole loop, not any one model. Yet no standard benchmark captures time-aligned, multi-layer traces of how drift and failures propagate across these layers, so we cannot diagnose where coordination breaks down, why, or how to recover. This paper targets one facet of that gap: drift, a deviation that can originate in any stack layer and that conventional single-modality monitoring cannot localize to a layer or pin to an onset time. We construct a benchmark by injecting controlled drift into traces derived from ALFRED, a grounded-instruction benchmark for everyday household tasks, yielding 1,918 drifted traces. Each trace is a time-aligned sequence of per-step records across five execution layers (state, observation, decision, rules, control), labeled with the drift type, affected layer, onset time, responsible actor, and causal mechanism, and validated by independent raters with inter-annotator agreement reported. We pair the dataset with a leak-aware protocol that removes a near-perfect onset leak, and a baseline study across classical, recurrent, and attention-based model families. Under this honest protocol, drift is identifiable and attributable well above random and majority baselines across every family (affected layer macro-F1 near 0.70, responsible actor near 0.85, causal mechanism near 0.49), and heavy attention offers no advantage over simpler models on this symbolic benchmark.

</details>


### [127] [CellWorld: From Gene-Level Reconstruction to Latent Cell Prediction in Spatial Transcriptomics Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06659)
*Haiping Liu,Qian Zhao,Lijing Lin,Jingyuan Sun,Hongpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CellWorld是一种针对空间转录组学的潜在空间预测预训练方法，通过预测掩蔽细胞的潜在表示而非直接重建基因测量值，在46M人类细胞语料上训练了4个不同规模的变体（5.74M–94.56M参数），在11个线性探针基准和7个微调空间基准上全面超越所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间转录组基础模型主要通过重建掩蔽基因身份或表达值进行预训练，这可能会鼓励模型复制实验特定的技术变异，从而限制表征的可迁移性。作者试图通过改变预测目标来避免直接重建此类技术变异。

Method: CellWorld将预测目标从观察到的基因测量值转移到潜在细胞表示，从可见的空间上下文和有限的局部表达提示中预测掩蔽细胞的潜在表示。作者在包含46M人类细胞的语料上预训练了4个变体（5.74M至94.56M可训练参数），并进行了受控缩放实验。

Result: 性能随模型容量提升，尤其在空间任务上；空间迁移更依赖充分的优化和广泛的生物来源多样性而非仅靠细胞数量。即使最小的CellWorld-Small（5.74M参数）也在所有11个线性探针基准和7个微调空间基准上超越所有基线。仅在语料5%上预训练的冻结CellWorld-Large在全部7个空间基准上超越所有全量微调基线。

Conclusion: 潜在空间预测预训练为空间转录组基础模型提供了可扩展的路径，能够有效避免重建技术变异带来的限制，并在极小模型和有限数据下仍展现优越的迁移性能。

Abstract: This paper shows that latent-space predictive pretraining can provide a scalable route to foundation models for spatial transcriptomics. Existing spatial transcriptomics foundation models primarily reconstruct masked gene identities or expression values, potentially encouraging the reproduction of assay-specific technical variation and limiting representation transferability. To avoid directly reconstructing such variation, we shift the prediction target from observed gene measurements to latent cell representations and introduce CellWorld, which predicts the latent representations of masked cells from visible spatial context and a limited partial-expression hint. We pretrain four CellWorld variants, spanning 5.74M to 94.56M trainable parameters, on a corpus of 46 million human cells. Our controlled scaling experiments show that performance improves with model capacity, particularly on spatial tasks, while spatial transfer depends more on sufficient optimization and broad biological source diversity than on cell count alone. Across four held-out datasets, even CellWorld-Small, with 5.74M trainable parameters, outperforms every baseline on all 11 linear-probe benchmarks and all seven fine-tuned spatial benchmarks. Most notably, a frozen CellWorld-Large pretrained on only 5\% of the corpus with broad biological source coverage outperforms every fully fine-tuned baseline across all seven spatial benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/UoM-HealthAI/CellWorld.

</details>


### [128] [Vehicle routing problem using deep reinforcement learning - A case study about truck planning in the industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06668)
*Siliang Lu,Dan Hu,Lili Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了深度强化学习在车辆路径问题（VRP）中的应用，通过三个不同的物流场景案例（外部卡车网络设计），展示了基于DRL的路径优化方案相比基线方法可实现超过10%的总成本优化，并展望了DRL算法在更多VRP变体中的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界中的VRP问题因约束复杂、信息不透明、环境不确定及人类非理性行为等因素，使得传统数学模型在实际部署中难以兼顾优化效果和可操作性。因此需要探索更智能的优化方法以有效应对实际物流场景中的各类复杂挑战。

Method: 本文采用深度强化学习（DRL）框架实现车辆路径优化，并通过三个不同的工业案例（涉及外部卡车网络设计）来验证方法的有效性。DRL智能体通过与仿真环境交互，学习最优路径决策策略。

Result: 实验结果表明，经过DRL智能体优化的路径相比基线方案可实现超过10%的总成本降低，证明了深度强化学习在实际物流场景中车辆路径优化的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文验证了深度强化学习在工业VRP场景中的可行性，并提出未来研究可将DRL算法推广到更多VRP变体中，进一步拓展其适用范围。

Abstract: As an important component of the supply chain industry, transportation has experienced rapid development in the past decade with the assistance of digital platforms and intelligent algorithms. Within the field of transportation research, Vehicle Routing Problem (VRP) has remained a persistent and enduring challenge. In the realm of management science, experts, and scholars from both the industrial and academic sectors have continuously explored optimization models and algorithms to effectively address routing problems, from the classical Traveling Salesman Problem to the more general Vehicle Routing Problem. These models and algorithms are applied in real-world industrial scenarios to achieve cost optimization and reduce carbon footprints. However, due to the complexity of real-world problems, numerous specific constraints are often added, and challenges such as information opacity, uncertainty, and irrational human behavior may arise. Therefore, deploying and optimizing mathematical models for VRP in practical scenarios while maintaining optimal results poses numerous challenges. This paper discusses and provides solutions for three different logistic use cases involving external truck network design. Through these industrial case study, the paper introduces how deep reinforcement learning-based vehicle routing optimization has been implemented. As a result, it can be observed that the routes optimized by reinforcement learning agent have over 10% total cost compared to baseline results. Furthermore, the paper proposes that in future research, DRL algorithms for vehicle routing problems could be generalized into more variations of VRP.

</details>


### [129] [A Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Coarse-Grained Molecular Dynamics of Polymers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06694)
*Joohee Choi,Junhyeong Lee,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CGMas是一个多智能体框架，利用大语言模型自动化聚合物粗粒化建模，从自然语言描述出发自动完成拓扑构建、平衡、映射、势函数推导和验证，在27个均聚物和共聚物任务中全部成功，22个任务与全原子密度误差在5%以内，并将仿真时间从38-88分钟缩短至1分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗粒化分子动力学能突破全原子方法在聚合物模拟中的尺度限制，但自下而上的粗粒化建模过程繁琐且劳动密集。粗粒化分辨率是一种设计选择，通常不存在可迁移的参数集，每种聚合物的映射都需要从头推导势函数，缺乏自动化工具。

Method: CGMas采用多智能体架构：大语言模型推理智能体从聚合物名称推断全原子拓扑结构；分层自校正机制解决不饱和、含杂原子和极性聚合物中常见的物理错误；下游智能体依次完成系统平衡、映射到粗粒化表示、通过玻尔兹曼反演推导势函数，并对照全原子参考验证模型性能。

Result: CGMas成功完成了全部27个均聚物和共聚物任务，其中22个任务的全原子密度匹配误差在5%以内，仿真时间从38-88分钟缩短至1分钟。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了智能体化的大语言模型是实现聚合物粗粒化自动化的有效途径，显著降低了粗粒化建模的人工成本和时间开销。

Abstract: Coarse-grained (CG) molecular dynamics extends polymer simulation beyond the scales accessible to all-atom (AA) methods, but bottom-up CG modeling is laborious. The CG resolution is a design choice, so a transferable parameter set is generally not available and the potentials are derived anew for each polymer mapping. Here we present CGMas, a multi-agent framework that automates topology construction, equilibration, mapping, potential derivation, and validation from a natural-language specification of the polymer and target resolution. A large-language-model (LLM) reasoning agent infers the AA topology from polymer name, while layered self-correction resolves physical errors common to unsaturated, heteroatom-containing, and polar polymers. Downstream agents equilibrate the system, map it onto CG representation, derive potentials through Boltzmann inversion, and benchmark the model against its atomistic reference. CGMas completed all 27 homopolymer and copolymer tasks, matched the AA density to within 5% in 22, and reduced simulation from 38-88 min to 1 min, establishing agentic LLMs as a route to automated polymer coarse-graining.

</details>


### [130] [AgentPatch: Coarse-to-Fine Weak-Task Repair for Merging Agentic Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06699)
*Zibo Shao,Baochen Xiong,Chengdong Xu,Linhui Xiao,Kaichen Li,Haoran Gong,Yan Li,Yaguang Song,Xiaoshan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentPatch，一个无需训练的粗到细修复框架，用于解决Agentic多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）合并中的两大挑战：非对称能力保留和行为关键遗忘。AgentPatch通过选择稳定的合并骨干、恢复稀释的弱任务信号（Weak-Task Unique Residual Recovery）以及应用行为关键补丁（Agent-Guided Behavior-Critical Patch），生成单一静态检查点（无需路由或集成），在六个智能体和多模态基准上提升了多样合并骨干的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Agentic MLLM模型针对特定工具或环境高度专业化，难以合并成一个通用模型。模型合并过程中存在两个核心问题：一是不同交互复杂度的能力在合并不均衡保留，导致弱任务产生；二是丢失关键决策行为会破坏长程执行流程。这些问题阻碍了单一通用Agentic MLLM的构建。

Method: AgentPatch采用无需训练的粗到细修复流程：(1) 选择稳定的合并骨干模型；(2) 通过Weak-Task Unique Residual Recovery恢复被稀释的弱任务特定信号；(3) 应用Agent-Guided Behavior-Critical Patch，在显式能力保护下恢复关键决策行为。最终输出为单一静态检查点，不需要路由机制或集成方法。

Result: 在六个Agentic和Multimodal基准上的实验表明，AgentPatch能够改善多种合并骨干模型的表现，缓解弱任务退化问题，并在弱任务恢复与保留互补的搜索能力和Agentic视觉处理能力之间实现更好的平衡。

Conclusion: AgentPatch作为一种训练免费的模型合并修复框架，能够有效缓解Agentic MLLM合并中的能力非对称保留和行为关键遗忘问题，输出单一静态检查点的设计使其便于实际部署，代码已开源。

Abstract: Agentic multimodal large language models (MLLMs) extend multimodal perception and reasoning with planning, tool use, and interaction in dynamic environments. Yet current models are specialized for particular tools or environments, complicating consolidation into a single generalist. We formulate Agentic MLLM Merging and identify two challenges: asymmetric capability preservation, whereby capabilities with different interaction complexity are retained unevenly, producing weak tasks after merging, and behavior-critical forgetting, whereby losing decisive actions can derail long-horizon execution. We propose AgentPatch, a training-free coarse-to-fine repair framework. It selects a stable merged backbone, restores diluted weak-task-specific signals through Weak-Task Unique Residual Recovery, and applies an Agent-Guided Behavior-Critical Patch that recovers decisive behaviors under explicit capability protection. AgentPatch produces a single static checkpoint without routing or ensembles. Experiments across six agentic and multimodal benchmarks show that AgentPatch improves diverse merged backbones, alleviates weak-task degradation, and better balances weak-task recovery with the preservation of complementary search and agentic visual processing capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/ziboshao/AgentPatch.

</details>


### [131] [WebRider: Persona-Conditioned Intent Controllers for Live-Web Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06704)
*Zhi Li,Tao Zhou,Yeqing Li,Eugene Ie,Demetri Terzopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WebRider提出将委派式Web任务中的"策略"形式化为意图契约（intent contract），通过分层架构（控制器-中间层-工具层）确保任务执行在目标、约束、证据义务和人格一致性上得到审计与保障，并配套提出RiderBench基准（4,096个实时网页契约、42个网站）验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实时网页智能体仅以最终答案作为评估标准，忽略了委派过程中必须遵守的策略约束（验证什么、如何处理不确定性、哪些偏好重要、何时停止）。全面审计显示强控制器虽能完成99.2%的任务，但仅有38.8%的案例完全遵守所有策略约束——"完成"并不意味着"忠实"。这暴露了当前评估体系的关键缺口。

Method: WebRider将委派策略形式化为意图契约（intent contract），作为目标、约束、证据义务、答案形式和任务本地人格控制的运营记录。采用分层架构：顶层控制器维护契约状态，中层将意图实现为带守卫的可执行动作，工具层通过浏览器、搜索和地图工具执行动作。RiderBench基准在42个公共网站的4,096个实时契约上审计内部契约状态和可见用户体验。中间守卫接口同时作为高质量训练信号，训练8B动作策略模型。

Result: RiderBench在4,096个实时网页契约上验证了该设计，审计契约内部状态与可见用户体验以判断rollout是否保持策略及步骤是否人格一致。通过守卫中间接口训练的8B动作策略模型在固定控制器下优于仅可执行动作的基线。
